#### 5.13 Phase 5 Study Record — Bounded Documentation Study (2026-08-21)

**Record status**: **Documentation-only study complete; no implementation authorized** (LOCK-P5-001). **Main SQLite authority, stable-ID anchors, `sort_order`→`id` intra-response ordering, renderer-local generation applicability-only, no `fromGeneration` delta cursor** (LOCK-P5-002). **Phase 5 exit and all three gates remain Open** (LOCK-P5-003). **`architecture.md` and governance owners unchanged** (LOCK-P5-004). **No deferred payload/channel/SQL/window-size/cap/eviction granularity choice selected** (LOCK-P5-005).

**Current surfaces (observed — not target)**:
- Topic load: `loadTopicMessagesThunk` → `dbService.fetchMessages` → typed IPC → `ChatDbAggregateService.fetchMessages` **one Main transaction** invoking `MessagesRepository.listByTopic` / `BlocksRepository.listByMessages` → renderer **whole-topic** projection (single source for viewport/context/groups).
- Consumers assume whole-topic resident (`computeContextInfo`, `reconcileMessageWindow`, display grouping); no windowed/incremental/replacement path.
- Dense `sort_order` rewritten by middle insert/delete/reorder; deterministic order is `sort_order`→`id` only within one authority transaction; no revision/snapshot/linearizability.
- No windowed fetch (`around anchor/message`, latest window), no authority-side context closure window; context anchor-to-end computed in renderer from whole-topic.
- Empty-topic completeness is implicit (`cachedIds.length > 0` early-return), not explicit empty-topic marker; current `dbService.fetchMessages` (messages/blocks only) plus separate/unawaited segment load does not satisfy target R-01 `whole-topic` (chat-data ∧ segment) resident completeness.
- Failures per-fetch; no single-flight/serialize contract enforced at data-access layer.

**R-02..R-06 matrix (target contract, not implementation)**:

| Intent | Coverage target | Completeness produced | Counting unit | Anchor stability | Generation interaction |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R-02 Latest window | Latest N in deterministic order | `window` (tail N, bounds declared) | complete rendered/message groups | topic ID stable | new generation → re-anchor latest |
| R-03 Window around stable anchor | Neighborhood of anchor (±K) | `window` (around anchor, bounds declared) | complete rendered/message groups | anchor message ID stable | structural mutation → old window not trusted |
| R-04 Window around message (search-hit) | Around hit message | `window` (around hit, bounds declared) | complete rendered/message groups | hit message ID stable | hit deleted → not-found |
| R-05 Answer-group window | All members of answer group, authority-resolved | `answer-group` (member set declared) | messages in complete group | message/group ID stable | membership may change → re-resolve from authority |
| R-06 Context closure | Anchor-to-end closed range (stable anchor through newest, independent of current `contextCount`; `contextCount` is initialization/re-anchor provenance only) | `context closure` (anchor-to-end, bounds declared) | complete context turns | anchor ID stable (renderer-owned) | structural mutation → re-close; `contextCount` change alone does not resize/invalidate |

Ordering for all: `sort_order`→`id` within single authority transaction; no tuple-cursor stability across mutations; no revision/snapshot/linearizability; stale generation discarded before any join/action; no `fromGeneration` delta cursor (former R-07 removed; deferred to Phase 6 without generation authority cursor, §5.1/§5.4/§5.5).

**Coordinated review surfaces (implementation requires stop + review before any code)**:
- Shared IPC/preload/cross-process contract: any new read intent (R-02..R-06), payload-shape/completeness-typing change, or authority-aware target action (group resolve, anchor-based positioning, hit-window) → coordinated shared-contract review across shared types/channel definitions, preload exposure, and Main handlers; architecture/program approval; formal ADR only when governance requires; both-side edits mandatory (§5.11 / §10.1).
- Persistence/migration/schema: any revision/snapshot/tombstone/column or `sort_order` semantics change → SQLite migration governance (ADR).
- Context-window semantics: any anchor/`contextCount`/repair change → `context-window.md` review.
- Identity/compatibility, release/platform, native/multi-window, sync boundary per §10.1 — no change introduced here.

**Test implications (contract-level, no implementation test claimed)**:
- **L1 (correctness gates, design)**: typed completeness present on every response (no masquerading; `window` bounds declared; empty explicit); `sort_order`→`id` ordering verified per response; stable-ID re-anchor after structural mutations (no tuple-cursor carry); generation applicability stale-discard before any join/action; authoritative-deletion invalidation of all completeness types before any join/action; answer-group authority-resolved; context closure separate from viewport window.
- **L3 (directional observation only)**: window/closure hit/miss, miss latency, canonical logical bytes, generation advance/stale-discard, single-flight/serialize depth, scroll/viewport/context metrics, pinned vs evictable working-set logical bytes + heap-amplification ratio (§5.10). No threshold/baseline/improvement claim; L3 is calibration input for Phase 6.
- Current test surfaces remain **whole-topic** (`fetchMessages` path); windowed/closure/group paths are **contract-only** and have no implementation test coverage in this record. Future Phase 6 implementation must add focused contract tests per intent and generation-scoped cache-join tests.

**Open gates (exit remains Open, §5.12)**:
1. **Coordinated shared-contract review** of target read intents (R-02..R-06) and authority-aware actions — **partially satisfied at that record time**: executed and documented for R-02/R-03 (§5.14) with scoped direction approval (§5.14a); **at that time not satisfied for R-04..R-06**, which remain unreviewed and unapproved — historical statement scoped at that time; current status coordinated-review precondition **executed/documented and direction-approved for R-02..R-06** via §5.15/§5.15a (see §5.15 explicit result and §5.15a approval; calibration and §5.10 remain Open) *(parenthetical current-status clarification; historical text preserved)*.
2. **Calibration** of window/closure/pinned-working-set sizing under Phase 4 B-01/B-02 canonical logical-bytes + heap-amplification and unlimited-context measurement — not performed.
3. **Data-access acceptance criteria validation** per §5.10 (scoped window/closure reads with defined counting units, typed completeness with validated semantic derivation, deterministic `sort_order`→`id` with stable-ID anchoring and no generation-based deltas, separate viewport/context projections with renderer-owned anchor, pinned streams with structural vs content-only invalidation, deferred window eviction granularity with no partial-entity eviction within admitted semantic unit) — not demonstrated.
- Phase 3 remains **Structurally Closed 2026-08-21**; Phase 4 exit remains **Open** for independent calibration/implementation; PERF-TOPIC-SWITCH/PERF-ECHO remain **Open as independent post-refactor workstreams (non-blocking, ARCH-011)**; Phase 6 remains **unauthorized candidate**.

**Preserved boundaries and non-claims**:
- ARCH-001..008 unchanged (§1.2/§1.3 and §5.1 row 1 cites ARCH-008; §5.11 sync review); no governance change; sync prohibitions preserved (ARCH-006).

- R-01 `whole-topic` remains **chat-data ∧ segment for same generation** (explicit empty markers, jointly validated, published atomically); chat-data/segment alone are component/staging markers, not new R intents; `window` never satisfies `whole-topic`; generic `whole-topic` label never implicitly satisfies `answer-group`/`context closure` without derived/validated closure (§5.3/§5.6).

#### 5.14 Coordinated shared-contract review record — R-02/R-03 window reads (2026-08-22)

**Review status**: **Review-only; documentation-only.** This review is performed per the program's mandatory stop/review obligation for any new read intent: the coordinated shared-contract review must span all three required surfaces jointly — shared types/channel definitions, preload exposure, and Main handlers — plus, for gap assessment, the renderer consumption layer that would receive the result. This record executes that review against current reality for the R-02 latest-window and R-03 around-anchor window intents and documents it as evidence. It **adopts nothing and selects nothing**: no payload field, channel name, SQL clause, N/K/window-cap value, or eviction granularity is chosen, and no implementation is authorized. The coordinated-review precondition of the data-access exit gate is hereby executed and documented; **explicit architecture/program approval remains outstanding, and all Phase 4/5 exits remain Open**.

**Table A — Current-reality inventory** (observed structure only, not target):

| Layer | Current state | Reference |
|---|---|---|
| Shared channel/type | `chatdb:fetch-messages`; request carries `{ topicId }` only; response is `{ messages, blocks }` JSON arrays | `packages/shared/chatDb/types.ts:87-89`, `258-262`, `635` |
| Shared validation | Allowed-keys admits exactly `topicId`; any added request key is rejected at the boundary; result profile caps strings and the blocks aggregate | `packages/shared/chatDb/contracts.ts:139-145`, `146-183` |
| Preload | Forwards the request unchanged on `window.api.chatDb.fetchMessages` | `src/preload/index.ts:625-626` |
| Main handler | Per-call aggregate service, single root transaction per invocation | `src/main/services/chatDb/ipc.ts:262-265`; `ChatDbAggregateService.ts:100-133` |
| Repository SQL | Both queries return the full topic's rows (messages filtered by `topic_id`; blocks selected via `message_id IN (topic's message IDs)`), `ORDER BY sort_order ASC id ASC`, no LIMIT; composite indexes `(topicId, sortOrder)` / `(messageId, sortOrder)` back them | `repository/MessagesRepository.ts:159-167`; `repository/BlocksRepository.ts:89-105`; `schema/index.ts:52,75` |
| Renderer thunk | Cache early-return infers completeness from non-empty cached IDs; publishes blocks then wholesale-replaces the topic ID list; segments loaded separately and unawaited | `store/thunk/messageThunk.ts:1525-1590` |
| Projection stores | `messagesReceived` replaces the ordered ID list (replace-not-merge); blocks upsert-many | `store/newMessage.ts:133-139`; `store/messageBlock.ts:82-91` |
| Windowing on the read path | Absent end-to-end — no window parameter in the request type, validator, preload surface, or SQL | Rows above |
| Dormant pagination machinery below contract | Repository-level keyset pagination over `(sort_order, id)` with typed cursors; unit-test callers only; no aggregate/IPC exposure | `repository/MessagesRepository.ts:169-208`; `domain/cursor.ts:24-83` |

**Table B — Requirement status checklist** (status against current reality; evidence basis cites Table A rows):

| Requirement | Status against current reality | Evidence basis |
|---|---|---|
| Ordering authority `sort_order`→`id` | Satisfied at SQL level for both queries | A5 |
| Stable-ID anchoring | Structurally available (IDs are primary keys); tuple-cursor machinery exists but design excludes it as anchor | A9 + intent norms |
| Typed `window` completeness | Absent; response carries no marker or bounds | A1 |
| Declared bounds in response | Absent | A1 |
| Counting unit of complete rendered/message groups | Not representable today (whole-topic only; group reconstruction happens renderer-side) | A7 |
| No window masquerading as whole-topic | Trivially satisfied while only whole-topic intent exists; consumer inference mechanism absent | A6/A7 |
| Segment component not required unless joined | Current separate/unawaited segment load is compatible; coupling flagged | A6/A7 |
| Generation applicability tagging | Absent on the wire; renderer-local transition epochs exist and are semantics-compatible (applicability-only) | A6 |
| Explicit not-found / empty-topic distinction | Gap: an absent topic currently returns empty arrays | A4 |
| Coverage-check cache join (bounds fully cover requested range) | Gap: early-return uses a non-empty-IDs heuristic | A6 |
| Re-anchor by stable ID after structural mutation / bounds-not-cursors | No mechanism; behaviorally safe today because only whole-topic intent exists | A6/A9 |
| Concurrent same-topic window reads serialize | N/A until windowed reads exist | intent norms |

**Findings**:

1. **F1 — Any windowed read is necessarily a coordinated both-side contract change.** A request extension is rejected by the frozen allowed-keys validator; types/channels/preload/handlers are single-file chokepoints that also serve mutation and streaming contracts (`types.ts` command map; the IPC channel enum block; the single preload literal bridge; the generic handler wrapper in `ipc.ts`). Shared contract tests pin fetch-messages behavior and would be edited together with the contract.
2. **F2 — Main-local dormant keyset pagination seam exists unused.** It has test-only callers. The design explicitly makes no sufficiency claim for it under concurrent dense-order mutation and excludes tuple cursors as anchors; it is infrastructure precedent, not a conformant contract primitive.
3. **F3 — Envelope precedent exists on adjacent channels.** Trash listing accepts limit/cursor; search accepts pageSize/cursor and returns nextCursor/hasMore. Recorded as fact, not a selection.
4. **F4 — Consumer-side collision.** The wholesale-replace reducer and the non-empty-IDs completeness heuristic cannot install or reason about partial windows; remedy is deferred to implementation, not selected here.
5. **F5 — Secondary direct call sites bypass the thunk assuming whole-topic semantics** (`services/SpanManagerService.ts:143`; `pages/history/components/SearchResults.tsx:390`) plus `TopicManager.getTopicMessages` and HistoryPage dispatch sites. This is a migration-surface inventory for any future slice, not defects today.
6. **F6 — `getRawTopic` duplicates the read shape non-transactionally.** Its scope divergence is undocumented; listed for a future implementation-scope decision.

**Carried-forward unknowns**:

- Whether mini/trace-viewer windows dispatch the load thunk at runtime was not traced.
- The intended consumer of the dormant pagination seam is unstated by any comment.

**Findings**:

1. **F1 — Adjacent typed mutation and renderer-local window must be reconciled in any future authority-aware design.** `chatdb:select-answer-message` is a typed MUTATION with caller-supplied full group; R-04's window is renderer-local from resident state. Any future typed READ for R-04/R-05 must define the authority boundary without reinterpreting the mutation's caller-responsibility contract. Recorded as fact, not a selection.
2. **F2 — Two divergent R-04 consumer paths exist, both projection-only.** History search whole-topic client-find and main-window resident-window build (10 older / 19 newer groups) are separate code paths with separate not-found handling. A future around-message window must supersede both without adopting a window value.
3. **F3 — Fetch path doubles as read-with-write.** The current `fetchMessages` path auto-primes an absent topic, so deleted-hit, empty-topic, and not-yet-loaded cannot be distinguished by authority today.
4. **F4 — Answer-group closure is entirely renderer-side.** Main never resolves `askId` groups; branch/clone/edit remain client positional-index based. Any future stable-anchor positioning is a new authority surface, not an extension of current mutation handling.
5. **F5 — Stale resident-projection risk for positional windows.** Windows derived from resident Redux (A7) can diverge after mutation; cache-join and re-anchor semantics would need an authority check — not selected here.
6. **F6 — Dormant keyset pagination is test-only precedent.** Carried unchanged from §5.14: infrastructure precedent with test-only callers, not a conformant contract primitive; no sufficiency claim for tuple cursors as anchors under concurrent dense-order mutation.
7. **F7 — R-06 has no shared contract surface at all.** No types, channels, contracts, preload, or Main code for context closure; shared search yields only diagnostics labels. Context-window governance already fixes anchor ownership in renderer `AssistantSettings` — this record does not alter it.
8. **F8 — R-06 closure input diverges across consumers.** Four renderer-local sites share `computeContextInfo` but request-model closure truncates/filters messages differently from UI projection; not-found/empty sentinel handling and bounds/cache joins are renderer conventions, not shared-contract guarantees.

#### 5.16c Phase 4/5 Exit Decision & Remaining Acceptance Closure — 2026-08-25 (documentation-only; Phase 4 Open, Phase 5 Open; S6.4/S6.5 Candidate — not authorized)

> **Scope lock — durable governance record, not implementation, not closure**: This subsection is the **durable governance record** for the next phase: **Phase 4/5 Exit Decision & Remaining Acceptance Closure**. It **explicitly dispositions every §4.9 and §5.10 criterion** as `pass`, `partial`, `unverified`, or `not claimed`, and **classifies remaining gaps without authorizing new implementation**. The next phase is this exit-decision and remaining-acceptance closure; **S6.4 and S6.5 remain candidate/not authorized**, the Phase 4 cache is **not implemented**, no second main chat window is added or claimed, and no threshold, baseline, capacity, or product policy is adopted. No source code, tests, package files, schemas, IPC, migrations, `architecture.md`, or governance ADRs were altered by this record.

**Decision record — 2026-08-25**:

- **Phase 4 — Bounded Memory and Cache**: **Open**. Design study complete (2026-08-20) as documentation-only; calibration evidence is directional synthetic (C-01/pinned/C-02). No bounded evictable-cache enforcement, staged same-generation publication, TTL→oversized→LRU ordering, whole-topic atomic eviction, viewport/scroll/search bounds, or complete cache-miss/eviction observability has been demonstrated under exercised workload. Exit remains Open pending the true blockers listed below.
- **Phase 5 — Data-Access Contract**: **Open**. Design study complete (2026-08-20) as documentation-only; R-02..R-06 direction approved (§5.14a/§5.15a) and S6.1–S6.3 bounded slices are Authorized & Implemented, but full §5.10 observability, single-flight/serialize, complete generation/stale-discard, in-flight race, and eviction/closure-granularity acceptance remain incomplete. Exit remains Open pending the true blockers listed below.
- **Phase 6**: S6.1, S6.2, and S6.3 bounded slices remain Authorized & Implemented at `6fa20eb3869fe83e46be965d1991f98bd5fadc2a`; **S6.4 Index/query and S6.5 File dual-state/FTS dedup remain Candidate — not authorized**. No S6.4/S6.5 work is started by this record.
- **Window topology**: The supported product topology has **one main chat window** only; synthetic duplicate renderer evidence is **not** acceptance evidence.

**Disposition vocabulary** (used in the two tables below and in the exit matrix):

| Term | Meaning in this record |
|---|---|
| **pass** | Criterion satisfied for its declared scope with sufficient evidence for that scope; does not imply other criteria or full phase closure. A scoped `pass` (e.g., authority-aware actions for R-04/R-05) does not close its parent phase. |
| **partial** | Some scoped implementation or focused evidence exists (contract validation, bounded slice, focused suite, single-window E2E, or directional calibration), but full acceptance — including exercised-workload, integrated runtime, or complete observability — remains unproven. Partial does not satisfy an exit gate. |
| **unverified** | No implementation surface or acceptance evidence exists in this worktree for the criterion; behavior remains design/target only. Unverified is an exit blocker where the criterion is required for exit. |
| **not claimed** | Criterion intentionally not demonstrated or claimed in current scope; no evidence is presented as product acceptance (included for completeness and to prevent inference of implicit coverage). |

**Calibration defaults remain design only**: The Phase 4 capacity numbers **8 topics (B-01)**, **32 MiB aggregate logical retained payload (B-02, exact frame `phase4-logical-payload-v1` canonical UTF-8 JSON)**, **30-minute idle TTL (B-03)**, and related bounds (200-group viewport, 256-scroll, 500 Range) remain **architecture capacity defaults requiring calibration before implementation**. They are **not runtime policy, not thresholds, not baselines, and not validated product defaults**; they are referenced as **calibration candidates only** and no eviction, cache, or context-window enforcement is claimed.

##### Bounded decision (2026-08-25)

- **Phase 4 and Phase 5 remain Open.** No phase is declared pass; no Phase 4/5 exit is closed by this record.
- **No S6.4 or S6.5 work is started.** S6.4 Index/query and S6.5 File dual-state/FTS dedup remain **Candidate — not authorized**; any future S6.4/S6.5 candidate work requires a separate explicit authorization decision and, where schema/persistence is touched, governance/ADR.
- **No second main chat window is added or claimed.** The supported product topology has **one main chat window** only. A harness-created duplicate main renderer is **not a supported product topology** and **must not count as Phase 5 product acceptance evidence**; mocked `getAllWindows` broadcast evidence is **infrastructure evidence only**, not multi-main product evidence. Multi-window Electron deletion, in-flight race, and eviction/closure-granularity evidence remain open/deferred.
- **Phase 4 cache implementation remains gated.** No bounded-cache enforcement, stale-discard, or viewport/scroll/search bound is implemented by this record; any future Phase 4 cache implementation requires explicit authorization, and **8 topics, 32 MiB, and 30-minute TTL remain design calibration/defaults only**, not runtime enforcement or product policy.
- **Existing architecture and risk are recorded without implying product completion.** This record dispositions every §4.9 and §5.10 row conservatively; `pass` is scoped, `partial` does not satisfy exit, `unverified` and `not claimed` remain open by design. No capacity, threshold, baseline, performance guarantee, or eviction/window-size/cap/cursor value is adopted or selected; **all §4.9 and §5.10 rows are dispositioned above, explicit blocker/defer/authorization classification exists**, and remaining risks are listed below.

**Concrete remaining risks** (without authorizing implementation): Phase 4 unbounded renderer projection growth persists until bounded-cache enforcement is implemented; staged chat-data + segment same-generation publication remains unproven; TTL→oversized→LRU ordering and whole-topic atomic eviction remain unproven; viewport/scroll/ContentSearch bounds remain unenforced; cache-miss and eviction observability remains partial; Phase 5 generation/stale-discard and authoritative deletion require multi-window Electron plus in-flight race and eviction/closure-granularity runtime evidence; single-flight/serialize concurrency behavior is unproven and not claimed; C-01/pinned/C-02 remain synthetic directional calibration on single-machine profiles with GC-sensitive heap.

No ambiguity about section numbering required resolution beyond choosing the next preserved overlay number **§5.16c** to follow §5.16b; no lock challenges with evidence — the four coordination constraints were applied as documented without contradiction.

**Status**: **S6.1, S6.2 and S6.3 bounded slices (contract/Main §6.3, renderer cache-join §6.3.1, bounded anchor/viewport repair §6.3.2) Authorized & Implemented at HEAD `6fa20eb3869fe83e46be965d1991f98bd5fadc2a`; S6.4/S6.5 remain Candidate — not authorized.** Requires explicit approval and, where noted, governance/ADR before any code, schema, or IPC work for remaining slices. #### 6.1 Authorization and Implementation Record — S6.1 Windowed Reads R-02/R-03 (2026-08-22)

**Status**: **Authorized and Implemented in this worktree (2026-08-22)** — per-slice authorization state recorded here; Phase 4/5 exits remain **Open**; S6.2/S6.3 and all other Phase 6 slices remain **Candidate — not authorized**; no Phase 5/6 closure claimed.

**Authorization basis**: LOCK-S6.1-001..005 (scope S6.1 R-02/R-03 only; separate window channel preserving `chatdb:fetch-messages` whole-topic compat; discriminated `latest`/`around` requests with caller-provided bounded counts 1..100 per count; typed window metadata with distinct empty/missing semantics; one-transaction deterministic `sort_order ASC, id ASC` stable-ID anchoring); R-02/R-03 direction already approved via §5.14a; §5.14/§10.1 coordinated shared-contract review executed before implementation. No S6.2/S6.3, no R-04/R-05/R-06, no sync/migration/eviction/generation-cursor authorization.

**What was implemented (end-to-end, scoped to S6.1)**:
- **Shared contract**: `packages/shared/chatDb/types.ts` adds `FetchMessagesWindowRequest` (discriminated `latest {topicId,limit}` / `around {topicId,anchorMessageId,before,after}`) and `FetchMessagesWindowResponse` (`messages`/`blocks` + `window {kind,completeness:'window',topicId,anchorMessageId,requested,first/lastMessageId,returnedCount,hasMoreBefore/After}`) plus `ChatDbCommands` entry `chatdb:fetch-messages-window`; `packages/shared/IpcChannel.ts` adds `ChatDb_FetchMessagesWindow`.
- **Validation**: `packages/shared/chatDb/contracts.ts` adds `fetchMessagesWindowContract` with strict `allowedKeys` and per-kind bounded-count (1..100) validation, unknown-field rejection, and typed `window` result validation (no masquerading as `whole-topic`).
- **Main**: `src/main/services/chatDb/ChatDbAggregateService.ts` adds `fetchMessagesWindow` — one transaction, `sort_order ASC, id ASC`, stable-ID anchoring, complete message+block groups, `hasMoreBefore/After`, distinct empty vs. `NOT_FOUND` for missing topic/anchor; `src/main/services/chatDb/ipc.ts` registers the channel; `src/preload/index.ts` exposes `window.api.chatDb.fetchMessagesWindow`.
- **Renderer**: `src/renderer/src/services/db/SqliteMessageDataSource.ts` adds typed `fetchMessagesWindow` (one bridge call, `ChatDbResult` unwrap, transport propagation) plus `windowCoverage.ts` helpers `isWindowCovering`/`isExactWindowMatch` for current-generation coverage checks; `src/renderer/src/services/db/DbService.ts` proxies the method; existing `loadTopicMessagesThunk` whole-topic path remains as compatibility fallback.
- **Tests**: `packages/shared/chatDb/__tests__/window.test.ts` (contract valid/invalid, bounds, empty/missing distinction), `src/main/services/chatDb/__tests__/window.test.ts` (aggregate latest/around, ordering, block grouping, hasMore, empty/missing), `src/main/services/chatDb/__tests__/ipcWindow.test.ts` (IPC/channel validation), `src/renderer/src/services/db/__tests__/windowDatasource.test.ts` (unwrap/transport), `src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/windowCoverage.test.ts` (coverage helper).

**Preserved / not changed**: Main SQLite authority and deterministic order untouched; existing whole-topic `chatdb:fetch-messages` behavior and callers unchanged; no schema/migration; no sync infrastructure; no R-04/R-05/R-06, no answer-group/context-closure, no eviction policy, no authority generation cursor; `architecture.md` not edited to describe target state; existing logging/style conventions preserved.

**Bounds clarification**: Concrete S6.1 request bounds (`limit` for `latest`; `before`/`after` for `around`, each **1..100**) are **contract validation bounds** enforced by the shared validator — not product defaults, not eviction policy, not window-cap selection. No default is introduced; callers must supply bounded counts. No eviction, cache, or context-window governance is changed.

**Remaining open**: Phase 4/5 exits (calibration, §5.10 validation, §4.9 acceptance) remain Open; S6.2/S6.3 and R-04/R-05/R-06 remain Candidate/not authorized; any future window/closure/eviction sizing or incremental/delta work remains deferred.

#### §6.2a Bounded viewport window group correction (R-02/R-03/R-04) — 2026-08-23

**Purpose**: Correct Main `fetchMessagesWindow` to count complete rendered/message groups per §5.4/§5.10 (consecutive assistant messages sharing a non-empty `askId` form one group; all other messages are singleton groups, matching `getMessageGroupSemanticKey`/`createMessageViewportGroupModel`), while preserving all existing typed contracts, authority boundaries, and 1..100 validation bounds. No R-06/context closure, cache joins, eviction, pinned working-set, migration, sync, identity/release/platform, or new channel/field/SQL/cursor/window-size/cap/threshold semantics.

**Main fix**: `src/main/services/chatDb/ChatDbAggregateService.ts` — `fetchMessagesWindow` now computes groups from authoritative ordered messages (`sort_order ASC, id ASC`) using the same semantic key as the renderer, then for `latest` returns the requested number of tail groups and for `around` returns the anchor's entire group plus the requested number of complete groups before/after (clamped to topic head/tail). `hasMoreBefore`/`hasMoreAfter` are derived from group boundaries (not raw indexes). One transaction, deterministic order, stable-ID anchoring, complete message+block reconstruction, and distinct empty vs `NOT_FOUND` for missing topic/anchor are preserved.

**Tests**: `src/main/services/chatDb/__tests__/window.group.test.ts` — 8 focused Main tests prove multi-message `askId` group crossing an `around` boundary is not split, `latest` counts groups not raw messages, singleton behavior, empty/not-found, deterministic ordering, non-consecutive same `askId` forms separate groups, and block relation completeness. Existing `window.test.ts` (10 tests) remains singleton-compatible.

**E2E**: `tests/e2e/specs/conversation/window-grouped.spec.ts` — contract-level regression seeding a contiguous multi-message `askId` group and exercising `window.api.chatDb.fetchMessagesWindow` latest/around via the standard fixture/disposable profile/mock provider. `tests/e2e/specs/conversation/window-grouped-integrated.spec.ts` — **integrated** regression seeding a grouped topic with a complete multi-message group and a separate non-consecutive same-`askId` run, activating the real topic via the standard fixture and asserting rendered Redux/DOM projection for complete per-run membership, separate groups for the duplicate `askId`, and unique deterministic outer DOM ids; uses standard electron fixture, disposable profile, mock provider, approved `ensureTopic`/`pasteMessagesToTopic` pattern only; no new IPC contract; no hand-rolled onboarding/profile/mock setup; does not claim diagnostic observation as regression proof. If Validation runs either spec it requires a fresh `pnpm build` (Electron lane ABI 145) before `pnpm test:e2e`; diagnostic build evidence is not claimed as regression proof beyond the Main unit coverage until Validation executes the E2E.

**Renderer projection alignment (S6.2a correction — 2026-08-23)**: `src/renderer/src/pages/home/Messages/messageViewportProjection.ts` — `projectMessageViewportGroups` now preserves canonical `displayGroups` one-to-one (no cross-run merge/split). It iterates precomputed `displayGroups` newest-first, emits each group's messages oldest-first with existing viewport-local `index`, uses canonical `group.key` as output key (repeated non-consecutive `askId` runs remain separate with complete per-run membership). Visual outer order, per-group order, indexes, divider matching, and `stableGroupId` rendering keys preserved; no input mutation; no IPC/schema/context/cache change.

**Renderer DOM-id uniqueness & comment (S6.2a bounded cleanup — 2026-08-23)**: `src/renderer/src/pages/home/Messages/MessageGroup.tsx` — outer DOM id generation now uses stable group/message-membership identity (`deriveStableGroupId`, length-prefixed `:`→`-` and `|`→`_` sanitized, `message-group-` prefix) to ensure separate non-consecutive groups sharing the same `askId` never share an outer DOM id; deterministic per membership, valid unique DOM ids for all groups; inner message ids and React reconciliation keys unchanged. `src/renderer/src/pages/home/Messages/Messages.tsx` stale projection comment updated to describe canonical precomputed `displayGroups`, one-to-one projection, newest-first outer order, oldest-first inner order, viewport-local indexes, and canonical keys. Focused unit tests in `src/renderer/src/pages/home/Messages/__tests__/MessageGroup.test.tsx` updated and extended to assert deterministic unique DOM ids for duplicate-`askId` groups.

**Non-scope preserved**: No renderer authority workaround, no new Main anchor repair API, no `answer-group`/`context closure`/`window eviction`/capacity change, no schema migration, no payload/channel expansion. Phase 4/5 remain Open.

| Layer | Change | File / Channel |
|---|---|---|
| **Shared contract** | `FetchContextClosureRequest {topicId, anchorGroupKey}` and `FetchContextClosureResponse {messages, blocks, closure {completeness:'context-closure', topicId, anchorGroupKey, firstMessageId, lastMessageId, returnedCount}}` plus `ChatDbCommands` entry `chatdb:fetch-context-closure` | `packages/shared/chatDb/types.ts` |

| **Main authority** | `fetchContextClosure` — one transaction, deterministic `sort_order ASC, id ASC`, builds context turns (user starts turn keyed by id; consecutive assistant with matching non-empty askId joins; other rows singleton keyed by askId or own id), resolves anchorGroupKey in authority order (user id → assistant askId → non-user id), returns slice from first message of resolved turn through newest with complete blocks, `NOT_FOUND` for missing topic/unresolved anchor | `src/main/services/chatDb/ChatDbAggregateService.ts` |
| **IPC** | Registers `ChatDb_FetchContextClosure` via typed `handleCommand` | `src/main/services/chatDb/ipc.ts` |
| **Preload** | Exposes `window.api.chatDb.fetchContextClosure` | `src/preload/index.ts` |
| **Tests** | Shared contract 21 tests and Main aggregate 12 tests covering user-key, assistant askId, orphan/system own-id, complete anchor turn, anchor-to-newest ordering, no viewport truncation beyond 100, missing topic/anchor NOT_FOUND, block completeness, unknown/invalid rejection | `packages/shared/chatDb/__tests__/contextClosure.test.ts` + `src/main/services/chatDb/__tests__/contextClosure.test.ts` (plus updated `contracts.test.ts` channel/coverage lists) |

#### 6.3.1 Renderer Cache-Join Slice — R-06 (2026-08-23)

**Status**: **Authorized & Implemented in this worktree as second bounded R-06 slice (2026-08-23)** — renderer half adds typed `fetchContextClosure` to `MessageDataSource`/`SqliteMessageDataSource`/`DbService` via existing bridge/unwrap/cloneForWire, fail-closed `isValidContextClosureResponse` helper, dedicated per-topic closure cache (not viewport `MessageWindow`), staged block+cache publication, shared `computeContextInfo` fed from closure rows when valid with viewport fallback, viewport `displayGroups`/`displayCount`/`load-more` unchanged, `contextCount=null` not used as fetch bound, same-anchor/same-generation stale discard, no new schema/migration/sync. **What was implemented (renderer, additive, bounded)**:

| Layer | Change | File |
|---|---|---|
| **DB interface** | Typed `fetchContextClosure(request: {topicId, anchorGroupKey}): Promise<FetchContextClosureResponse>` one bridge call, unwrap, transport propagation, no retry/fallback | `src/renderer/src/services/db/types.ts` + `SqliteMessageDataSource.ts` + `DbService.ts` |
| **Validation helper** | `isValidContextClosureResponse(request, response)` fail-closed: rejects wrong completeness/topic/anchor, malformed first/last/count, missing anchor row per recognized-role priority (user id → assistant askId → assistant/system own id; null/tool/unknown never resolve), viewport `window` masquerading, block/messageId mismatch, **every empty anchor-based closure (returnedCount===0) regardless of null bounds** (LOCK-R06-004); no invented count bounds | `src/renderer/src/services/contextClosure.ts` |
| **Cache helper** | `isValidContextClosureCacheHit(topicId, anchorGroupKey, currentFp?, cachedFp?)` same-anchor coherent check plus optional fingerprint equality (LOCK-R06-006); `get/set/clear` + `computeClosureFingerprint`/`getCachedClosureFingerprint`/`setCachedContextClosureWithFingerprint` plus **per-topic generation** `loadGenerations`/`cachedGenerations`/`bumpAndInvalidate` plus **global block epoch** `globalBlockGeneration`/`getGlobalBlockGeneration`/`bumpGlobalBlockGeneration` (covers uncached topics with active fetches) and **centralized freshness-gated helper** `getFreshValidatedClosure(topicId, anchor, currentFingerprint?)` / `isValidFreshClosure` combining structural + anchor + generation + fingerprint in one gate (fail-closed); deterministic authoritative fingerprint (ordering/IDs/roles/askId/status/blocks) plus generation snapshot at cache time, cheap local, no persistence/IPC generation protocol | `src/renderer/src/services/contextClosure.ts` |
| **Closure cache** | Dedicated per-topic `Map<topicId, FetchContextClosureResponse>` + `Map<topicId, fingerprint>` + `Map<topicId, generation>` + **global block epoch** separate from `MessageWindow`; async load on topic activation after `ensureTopicAnchorEstablished` (renderer-owned `contextWindowAnchor` groupKey), `contextCount` never a fetch bound; same-anchor/same-generation validated via **centralized helper** plus **store middleware generation invalidation** (`newMessages/*` → bump+invalidate topic, `messageBlocks/*` → bump+invalidate all cached topics **plus global epoch bump covering uncached topics with active fetches** — covers same-length/outside-viewport mutations without new IPC); stale/in-flight discarded via per-topic seq + topic/anchor ref + store anchor re-read + **generation/fingerprint/global-epoch re-read immediately before `upsertManyBlocks` and cache publication (discard on mismatch, prevents stale overwrite of newer blocks, covers uncached in-flight race)**; staged publish validates together then `upsertManyBlocks` + cache+fingerprint+generation snapshot in one transition, no `messagesReceived` viewport expansion; fallback explicit fail-closed to viewport when freshness cannot be proven | `src/renderer/src/hooks/useContextClosure.ts` + `src/renderer/src/store/index.ts` (middleware) |
| **UI projection** | `Chat.tsx` shared `computeContextInfo` now sources from **freshness-gated** `getFreshValidatedClosure(topicId, anchor, currentFingerprint)` (structural+anchor+generation+fingerprint) — centralizes cache read, fail-closed to viewport; closure blocks subscribed via `selectMessageBlocksByIds` so `filterEmptyMessages` sees closure blocks; `MessageWindow` remains viewport-sourced; `contextCount=null` unlimited not a fetch bound; `contextCount` change with stable anchor does not refetch | `src/renderer/src/pages/home/Chat.tsx` |
| **Model request path** | `ConversationService.prepareMessagesForModel` and `baseCallbacks.onComplete` attempt **same centralized freshness-gated helper** `getFreshValidatedClosure(topicId, anchor, currentFingerprint)` before `computeContextInfo`; single resolver semantics preserved across UI and model path; fallback to viewport on miss/stale, no silent different-anchor substitution | `src/renderer/src/services/ConversationService.ts` + `src/renderer/src/services/messageStreaming/callbacks/baseCallbacks.ts` |
| **Tests (focused)** | `isValidContextClosureResponse` 14+ cases (wrong completeness/topic/anchor, malformed bounds, missing anchor row, window masquerading, block mismatch, **empty rejected regardless of null bounds**, **null/tool/unknown own-id rejected, valid user/assistant/system still accepted**); cache hit valid/mismatch/**empty rejected**/**fingerprint mismatch rejected**; unbounded 150-message closure valid (no 1..100 bound); `ConversationService` same-anchor hit vs fallback vs mismatched; `contextCount` stable-anchor no-refetch; `closureViewport` viewport unchanged (displayGroups 5 vs closure 20, unlimited not truncated); `SqliteMessageDataSource` bridge 6 tests; `contextClosureStale` stale/in-flight/unbounded/window checks **plus same-length mutation fingerprint invalidation and ordering/role mutation fingerprint**; `contextTurnService` 49 tests including **null/tool/unknown ignored for turn membership/anchor resolution**; existing `contextInfo` still pass | `src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextClosure.test.ts` + `contextClosureStale.test.ts` + `closureViewport.test.ts` + `ConversationService.closure.test.ts` + `contextTurnService.test.ts` + `src/renderer/src/services/db/__tests__/SqliteMessageDataSource.test.ts` |
| **Preserved** | Existing context-window tests, latest/around behavior, model request API semantics, i18n/logging/style; no second resolver, no anchor shape/repair change, no viewport `displayGroups`/`displayCount`/`load-more` change, no broad fallback, no schema/migration/sync/delta/capacity/eviction | — |

**Verification (focused, pinned Node v24.11.1/pnpm 10.27.0)**:

- `./node_modules/.bin/vitest run --project renderer src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextClosure.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextClosureStale.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/closureViewport.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/ConversationService.closure.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextTurnService.test.ts` — **PASS 21+6+2+4+49=82** (contextClosure 21, stale 6, viewport 2, ConversationService 4, contextTurnService 49); re-audit finder: empty rejected, unknown roles cannot resolve, fingerprint same-length mutation invalidates
- `pnpm native:run node -- vitest run --project renderer src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextClosure.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextClosureStale.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextClosureFreshnessGate.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextClosureUncachedRace.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/ConversationService.closure.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/closureViewport.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextTurnService.test.ts` — **PASS 92** (adds 7 freshness gate tests: consumer gate, outside-viewport generation invalidation, fingerprint mismatch, conservative fail-closed, in-flight race, bumpAndInvalidateAll **plus 3 uncached deferred-fetch global-epoch race tests: uncached block mutation discards stale publication, clean publish, global advances without cache**)
- `pnpm native:run node -- vitest run --project renderer src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextInfoService.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/db/__tests__/SqliteMessageDataSource.test.ts` — **PASS 160** (contextInfo 30, Sqlite bridge 130)

- Shared contract `packages/shared/chatDb/__tests__/contextClosure.test.ts` already rejects empty (21 tests); Main `src/main/services/chatDb/__tests__/contextClosure.test.ts` NOT_FOUND for unresolved anchor preserved

**Runtime UI/E2E**: No integrated E2E with standard fixture was run in this slice — harness can seed a topic and observe DOM/Redux via `tests/e2e/specs/conversation/windowed-message-reads.spec.ts` pattern, but closure DOM proof (contextBoundary inside `#messages` with closure anchor through newest) was not exercised in this worktree run; reporting exact missing runtime proof per spec (diagnostic `pnpm ui:observe` not claimed as regression proof). Viewport interactive behavior remains window-tested; closure interactive not yet E2E-covered.

**Remaining risks / lock challenges (post re-audit fix — corrected 2026-08-23)**:

- **Corrected freshness — generation + fingerprint + global epoch, centralized gate, in-flight guard**: Same-length mutations are now invalidated via **generation bump through store middleware** (`newMessages/*` per-topic, `messageBlocks/*` conservatively all cached topics **plus global block epoch covering uncached topics with active fetches**) which covers same-length/outside-viewport mutations that viewport fingerprint alone could not detect; **centralized `getFreshValidatedClosure` helper** combines structural + anchor + generation + optional fingerprint in one fail-closed gate and replaces direct structural-only reads in `Chat.tsx`, `ConversationService.ts`, and `baseCallbacks.ts`; **in-flight guard re-reads generation/fingerprint/global-epoch immediately before `upsertManyBlocks` and cache publication and discards on mismatch**, preventing stale responses from overwriting newer block entities including the **uncached in-flight race (global epoch advances even when no cache entry exists; fetch captures globalAtFetch and discards on `messageBlocks/*` during fetch)**. This was the focused correction for R-06 renderer closure freshness (audit blind spot fix) plus final blocker fix for uncached race. Viewport newest-missing check retained as conservative fallback but no longer sole signal; generation/global are local, cheap, no new IPC/persistence/sync.
- **Residual block-content-only**: Fingerprint covers ordering/IDs/roles/askId/status/blocks association, not pure block content (e.g., main_text edits without message `updatedAt`/status/blocks change). **Now mitigated by generation bump on every `messageBlocks/*` dispatch** (conservative invalidation of all cached closures), so a block content edit that goes through the Redux block path will bump generation and force refetch. Residual remains only if a block content edit bypasses Redux (direct DB write without dispatch) — not an expected path; would require block-content fingerprint or explicit block-generation to fully close, but per scope that is reported as residual rather than inventing a broad subsystem (closure block publication already validates block/messageId refs).
- Hook-level cache is in-memory `Map` plus local `useState` plus per-topic generation; multiple `Chat` mounts for same topic share Map/generation but local state diverges until next effect — acceptable for single main window, multi-window not exercised; store middleware ensures all mounts see consistent invalidation.
- `isValidContextClosureResponse` now uses recognized-role priority (user id → assistant askId → assistant/system own id) aligned with `buildContextTurns` and Main; unknown/null/tool roles cannot independently resolve as own-id anchors (validated). Edge duplicate groupKeys still conservative (helper may accept what Main would have rejected, but Main never returns such — fail-closed direction).
- Viewport truncation blind spot **corrected**: outside-viewport same-length mutations previously undetectable by viewport fingerprint are now caught by generation invalidation; fingerprint remains for visible same-length mutations and as secondary check. No E2E proves closure-driven `boundaryMessageId`/`anchorGroupKey` rendered inside `#messages` with same-anchor validation across reloads; Recommend adding `tests/e2e/specs/conversation/context-closure.spec.ts` using standard fixture, `ensureTopic`+`pasteMessagesToTopic`, topic activation, and Redux/DOM assertions for closure completeness vs viewport. No new IPC/shared contract field was introduced — correction stays renderer-local per lock.

#### 6.3.2 Bounded R-06 Anchor/Viewport Repair Correction — Valid Out-of-Viewport Preservation (2026-08-24)

**Purpose**: Preserve a valid renderer-owned `contextWindowAnchor` when the latest viewport does not contain it, while retaining exactly-once repair for missing/ghost anchors and keeping context-window governance unchanged. This is a bounded correction to the viewport-anchored repair regression observed in the focused E2E (`msg-00000` → `msg-00044`).

**Root cause**: `loadTopicMessagesThunk` publishes only the latest viewport rows (e.g., latest 20 of 50), then `ensureTopicAnchorEstablished` validated `isActiveAnchorResolvableInMessages` against that truncated Redux projection. A valid early anchor (e.g., `msg-00000`) outside the viewport was therefore treated as missing and recomputed deterministically from the viewport tail (e.g., `msg-00044`), violating LOCK-R06-002/004 and `docs/context-window.md` (valid anchor never recomputed; ghost/missing still repairs; empty remains anchorless).

**What was implemented (minimal, renderer-side only)**:

| Layer | Change | File |
|---|---|---|
| **Anchor repair helper** | `ensureTopicAnchorEstablished` is now `async Promise<void>`. After viewport check, an active anchor not found in the viewport probes authoritative Main via the existing typed `fetchContextClosure({topicId, anchorGroupKey})` read path (reuse, no new IPC payload). Success → valid out-of-viewport → preserve (no dispatch). `NOT_FOUND` (ChatDbResultError or code includes NOT_FOUND) → ghost → fall through to deterministic default repair. Transport/unknown failures → fail-closed preserve. Missing/non-active anchors skip the probe and repair directly. Empty topics still anchorless. | `src/renderer/src/services/anchorService.ts` |
| **Thunk ordering** | `loadTopicMessagesThunk` cached path and fetch path both `await ensureTopicAnchorEstablished` after publication and before early-return/segment load; `sendMessage` `await`s before queuing response; Messages branch listener `void`-fires the async helper (preservesExactly-once dispatch per topic). | `src/renderer/src/store/thunk/messageThunk.ts` + `src/renderer/src/pages/home/Messages/Messages.tsx` |
| **Tests (focused unit)** | `anchorService.test` adds 4 cases: valid early anchor outside 20-row viewport not recomputed (authority success), ghost outside viewport still repairs (NOT_FOUND), transport failure fails closed, missing anchor initializes without probe. Existing valid-viewport, ghost, non-active, empty, unknown-assistant tests preserved async. | `src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/anchorService.test.ts` |
| **E2E oracle** | `context-closure.spec.ts` adds immediate post-bootstrap assertion that the persisted early anchor (`msg-00000`) equals the seeded anchor after `activateTopicAndWaitForBootstrap` and before any closure/contextCount calls. This makes the regression a hard E2E failure if bootstrap mutates the anchor. Existing viewport/closure/NOT_FOUND/contextCount stability assertions preserved. | `tests/e2e/specs/conversation/context-closure.spec.ts` |

**Preserved / not changed**: Main SQLite remains ordinary chat authority via typed IPC; renderer owns anchor state; closure remains separate from viewport/displayGroups; exactly-once repair for genuinely missing/authority-unresolvable anchors; `contextWindowAnchor` shape, no second resolver, no viewport fallback, no new schema/sync/delta/capacity/IPC channel; existing R-02/R-03/R-04/R-05/window/closure freshness behavior unchanged; Main never repairs/persists anchor.

**Verification (focused, pinned toolchain)**: `pnpm native:run node -- vitest run --project renderer src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/anchorService.test.ts src/renderer/src/services/__tests__/contextWindowService.test.ts` and `pnpm native:run node -- vitest run --project renderer src/renderer/src/store/thunk/__tests__/messageThunk.anchorEstablishment.test.ts src/renderer/src/store/thunk/__tests__/messageThunk.cachedAnchorRepair.test.ts src/renderer/src/store/thunk/__tests__/messageThunk.windowS61.test.ts` plus `pnpm --offline exec tsc --noEmit --project tsc` statically listed via `pnpm native:check:*` (no full build/E2E in this session per scope). Full `pnpm build` + `pnpm test:e2e` for the updated `context-closure.spec.ts` remains Validation-owned.

**Status**: Bounded correction implemented and recorded here; **not Phase 5/6 closure** and no capacity threshold claimed; `docs/architecture.md` remains current reality only (ARCH-008).

### Phase 7: Startup Architecture

**Entry criteria**: Phase 2 complete (conversation ownership/lifecycle design enables lazy activation); explicit approval. Conversation-startup validation depends on relevant Phase 3 activation slice/completion; independent boot tracks can proceed after explicit approval.
**Content**:
- Validate and integrate the startup effects of Phase 3 conversation activation (ContentSearch on invocation, edit capability on mode activation, optional panels/drawers on opening).
- Optimize app boot services, Redux rehydration, Dexie initialization, SQLite cold open, bundle loading.
- Address background window lifecycle.

**Exit criteria**: Startup improvements validated by startup acceptance criteria (conversation-startup integration verified, boot service ordering optimized, bundle loading improved). The existing cold-open `<500ms` threshold is owned by the performance measurement contract (`performance-measurement.md` §7), not created by this program.
**Dependencies**: Phase 2 complete for conversation-related startup validation; Phase 3 conversation activation slice/completion for conversation-startup integration; app boot services are independent tracks.
**Startup/memory relationship**: This phase addresses startup through both conversation-startup validation (Phase 3 dependency) and independent boot optimization tracks. The boot tracks (Redux rehydration, Dexie, SQLite cold open, bundle loading, background windows) can proceed independently of conversation refactoring.

### Phase 8: Future Sync Decision

**Entry criteria**: Phases 2-5 provide sufficient structural foundation; explicit governance decision to pursue sync.
**Content**:
- Make vendor-neutral sync architecture decisions (cross-device authority, conflict resolution, transport).
- Assess and document remaining vendor-neutral sync-readiness decisions and validation requirements against ARCH-005; any production implementation requires separate explicit governance approval.
- Production sync implementation only after governance approval.

**Exit criteria**: Sync architecture decisions documented; sync-readiness validated against ARCH-005 qualities.
**Dependencies**: Phases 2-5 provide structural foundation; governance approval required.
**PowerSync interpretation**: ARCH-004 (PowerSync No-Go) is vendor/constraint-specific and must not become a target architecture constraint. The target architecture must be vendor-neutral.

The DB-health work (PERF-DB-HEALTH in `performance-workstreams.md`) is reclassified according to architecture program dependency splits:

### Independent diagnostics (on demand, require Main/user activation; no schema ADR needed)

| Diagnostic | Scope | Dependencies |
|---|---|---|
| **M1** | Middle/batch insert O(N) sort_order shift — controlled scale curve | None |
| **M2** | Short (<3 codepoint) LIKE full-table scan — S1/S2 corpus attribution | None |
| **M3** | Index/query opportunity — query-plan structural diagnosis | None |
| **M7** | Cold open/load path — DB volume impact attribution | None |
| **M8** | Backup/restore health — L3 archive metadata (**Planned/Open · Authorized & Harness Implemented 2026-08-24 (inactive by default, `M8_L3_ARCHIVE_BENCH=1` / `pnpm bench:m8-l3-archive`); **Executed 2026-08-24 on dirty=false HEAD `f995e634a5362b80c553e8ea5c2e4a438e9bb3ab` (Node v24.11.1/pnpm 10.27.0, Node ABI 137) producing gitignored schema-v1 directional L3 artifact `chatdb-m8-l3-archive-health-20260824-182549.733.json` (measurement-only, clean provenance); future invocations remain inactive by default · see §7.2 contract; harness is Node `main-native` (ABI 137) per §7.2, not a schema/ADR gate to run design**) | None — harness executed as measurement-only directional L3 on dirty=false HEAD `f995e634a` with gitignored artifact; still Planned/Open, no clean baseline/threshold/capacity/default/Phase 4/5/6 closure, no real relaunch/startup promotion proven, no S6.4/S6.5 authorization |

These are **read-only with respect to production/user state** (M8 synthetic owned temporary backup/restore writes inside its isolated `mkdtemp` root are permitted and owned per §7.2-3; all other diagnostics are read-only in both production and synthetic space). They do not require governance/ADR approval to run (no schema/authority change involved). However, PERF-DB-HEALTH remains `Planned` — each diagnostic requires explicit Main/user activation before execution (`performance-program.md` §6/§8). They do not activate implementation.

| Item | Scope | Dependencies |
|---|---|---|

| **M4** FTS storage deduplication | Volume and write-amplification measurement + potential schema change | ADR for any schema change |
| **M5** File dual-state consistency | Consistency convergence diagnostic | ADR if resolution touches schema or authority boundaries |
| **M6** Sync metadata gap | Schema impact analysis | ADR; analysis only, no schema changes |

**Provenance**: Clean HEAD `cadddadd54ffc86056abb76f683f9abddca4c1a3`, `dirty=false` for all three artifacts, `schemaVersion: 1`, Node ABI 137 (self-ensured). Host toolchain pinned Node v24.11.1/pnpm 10.27.0. No `PERF_PHASE_ATTR` overlay; no implementation, schema, index, SQL, IPC, preload, or migration change.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **M2** Short <3 codepoint LIKE full-table scan | `pnpm bench:search-stage` | Node ABI 137 | `chatdb-search-stage-50k-20260824-083445.844.json` | v1 | **5/5 passed** — ordered parity, no-duplicates, bridge-vs-search parity, samples complete (50 per fixture × 10 fixtures), counts stable |
| **M3** Index/query query-plan structural diagnosis | `pnpm bench:search-stage-plan` | Node ABI 137 | `chatdb-search-stage-plan-50k-20260824-083038.584.json` | v1 | **5/5 passed** — ordered parity, no-duplicates, repeat-determinism (2 planner runs per fixture), classifier-complete, classifier invariants |
| **M7** Cold open / load / write runtime | `pnpm bench:main:native` | Node ABI 137 | `chatdb-sqlite-runtime-20260824-083953.978.json` | v1 | **3/3 passed** — corpus parity, cold-open sanity, cold-open p95 <500 ms (pre-existing gate, LOCK-5.4.3) |

**Directional outputs (artifact-authoritative, not adopted)**:

- **M2** (50k corpus, 10 fixtures, 50 measured samples): Stage-attribution timings (collect/filter/fetch and fetch-prepare/execute) recorded as L3 directional per-fixture p50/p95/mean; all correctness gates passed; values are synthetic single-profile observations, **not thresholds**, and **do not prove index benefit**.
- **M3** (50k corpus, 10 fixtures, 2 planner runs): Query-plan structural classification — per-fixture EQP rows (SEARCH/SCAN/temp-btree) and EXPLAIN opcode families (OpenEphemeral/Sort/MakeRecord/IdxInsert/Seek) with repeat-determinism; classifier invariants hold (bucket sums equal totals, finite counts); no optimization selected.
- **M7** (synthetic 5 topics × 200 messages = 1k messages / 1100 blocks, 20 load samples, 50 write ops, 15 cold-open runs): **Cold-open p95 15.24 ms passes the pre-existing `<500 ms` gate** (the only adopted threshold per `performance-measurement.md` §7); message-load p50 19.92 ms / p95 23.23 ms and write p50 35.91 ms (mean 41.84 ms, 23.9 ops/s) are **directional at 1k synthetic scale**, single-machine only, not baselines.

**Coverage limits**: Single-machine synthetic diagnostics only — one 50k search corpus profile (M2/M3) and one 1k-message runtime profile (M7); no S2-scale, no multi-device, no churn/profile, no M1 insert-shift evidence in this record (**M1 harness implemented and executed separately as dirty-provenance L3 per 701eb4f — see `performance-workstreams.md` §2.4; this M2/M3/M7 record's scope excludes M1**); single synthetic profile does not generalize to production workload; heat/heap not covered beyond M7 cold-open/load/write.

**Why Phase 4/5/6 and S6.4/S6.5 remain Open despite this record**: The §4.9/§5.10 calibration and implementation-acceptance gates require retained-payload-distribution calibration, scoped window/closure/cache-join acceptance, and Phase 6 per-slice authorization. Passing single-profile clean-HEAD diagnostics demonstrates harness correctness and provides directional attribution only; it does not satisfy those gates, does not prove query/index benefit, and does not authorize S6.4/S6.5 (which remain **Candidate — not authorized**). Phase 4 exit remains Open, Phase 5 exit remains Open, Phase 6 exit remains Open; no capacity-default or threshold adoption by this record (ARCH-011; LOCK-P5-001..005).

M8 activation is now authorized and harness implemented (2026-08-24), **and the authorized command has now executed (dirty=false) producing the artifact above**; future invocations still require separate activation + env gate and remain inactive by default.

**2 — Existing production path (subject under measurement; no duplication)**
- **Backup**: future harness must invoke the existing local path `BackupManager.backup(null, fileName, destinationDir, false)` — existing `archiver`/`StreamZip`/SQLite validation and current filtering; no private `restoreDirect` call, no algorithm duplication.
- **Restore**: future harness must invoke the existing local path `BackupManager.restore(null, archivePath, { preExitCleanup })` — exercises actual validation + extraction + staged `.restore` database file and captures the terminal `app.relaunch`/`app.exit` request; **parity ordering is explicit**: **pre-staging validation failures** (archive safety, metadata shape, `chat.db` presence, excluded artifact absence, snapshot integrity) **require zero `relaunch`/`exit` requests**; **post-staging parity is performed inside the `preExitCleanup` callback** — `BackupManager.restoreDirect` calls `preExitCleanup` **before** `app.relaunch`/`app.exit`, so the contract puts staged parity inside that callback; **on callback parity failure, cleanup/relaunch must be blocked and no artifact emitted** (fail-closed, no partial artifact). **No real process relaunch/startup promotion is executed or proven**.
- **Static evidence lane note**: real behaviour requires a **Node `main-native` lane with real `better-sqlite3`/`archiver`/`StreamZip` and the production backup/restore path, mocking only at the module boundary: Electron `app.getPath`/`getVersion`/`relaunch`/`exit` plus, only as needed, the existing non-production seams `chatDbService`, `WindowService`, `WebDav`/`S3` (established production-test seams; `BackupManager` metadata creation calls `app.getVersion`). Behavioral lifecycle mocking remains limited and production algorithms remain real; restore correctness is limited to validation/extraction/staging + captured relaunch/exit request, not real promotion (LOCK-004).
- Harness stubs at that module boundary and records `relaunch`/`exit` calls; it must assert **exactly one `relaunch`+`exit` request on successful restore, zero on any parity/validation failure (including callback parity failure)**. **No real `userData`/profile is allowed**.

**3 — Lane and isolation (future harness contract)**
- **Lane**: `main-native` **Node lane ABI 137** with **real** `better-sqlite3`/`archiver`/`StreamZip` and the production backup/restore path; Electron `app.getPath`/`getVersion`/`relaunch`/`exit` and, only as needed, `chatDbService`/`WindowService`/`WebDav`/`S3` are the only permitted module-boundary mocks (see §7.2-2).
- **Owned isolated root**: each scenario and **each sample** owns a **fresh realpath-canonicalized `mkdtemp` root** `<root>/scenario-<suffix>/sample-<index>/` containing: synthetic data DB, synthetic `Data`/`Files` fixtures, IndexedDB/Local Storage fixtures, destination archive, and restore staging. **Per-sample fixture construction and per-sample archive/staging cleanup are outside timed intervals** (see §7.2-5). **M8 is not read-only in synthetic space — this owned temporary backup/restore I/O is permitted; read-only qualification applies only to production/user state.**
- **No live `chatDbService`/user profile**; use an **isolated initialized synthetic DB through the existing production-test boundary** (same boundary as `backupManager.production.test.ts`).
- **Exact path mapping (per sample root `<root>`)**: control Electron `app.getPath('userData')` to make `BackupManager.getDataPath()` resolve `<root>/user-data/Data` (`BackupManager` imports `getDataPath()` which derives as `<mocked app.getPath('userData')>/Data` per `src/main/utils/index.ts`; no `DATA_PATH` read in `BackupManager`); mock Electron `app.getPath('temp')` → `<root>/temp`; local destination → `<root>/destination`; archive → `<root>/destination/backup-<suffix>-<sampleIndex>.zip`; restore extraction is the **production-derived child under mocked `temp`**; staged `.restore` directories are **production-derived children under mocked `userData`**. **Before each public call, realpath-check all mapped roots and assert they are descendants of the owned sample root**; never use the real profile, real `userData`, or a shared temp root. `DATA_PATH` is only an **optional independent mock seam for `chatDbService`**, if needed, **never as a `BackupManager` path control**.

**4 — Scale matrix (bounded synthetic fixture inputs; declared numerically in `benchmark.scale`; S0/S1 only; closed schema v1)**
- Three bounded synthetic scenarios — fixture inputs, **not** observed DB/archive-size claims; documented as **S0/S1 only (no S2/S3)**:
  - `M8-S0`: `topics=1, messages=100, blocks=100, fileEntries=4, fileBytes=4096`
  - `M8-S1`: `topics=10, messages=1000, blocks=1000, fileEntries=32, fileBytes=65536`
  - `M8-S1-wide`: `topics=25, messages=5000, blocks=5000, fileEntries=128, fileBytes=262144`
- Each scenario runs with **`skipBackupFile=false` only**; the asymmetry of `skipBackupFile` is **out of M8 scope and must be stated as not measured**.
- **Closed schema v1 mapping (no arbitrary `scenario` field)**: `benchmark.scale` declares **numeric `scenarioCount=3`**; scale rows above are the **fixed per-scenario scale** identified by the **metric/gate ID suffix** `s0` (`M8-S0`), `s1` (`M8-S1`), `s1wide` (`M8-S1-wide`). No free-form `scenario`/`path`/`content` field is permitted. `benchmark.scale` fields and `metrics[].value` are finite numeric; `gates[].passed` is boolean and `gates[].detail` when present is bounded non-empty string with M8 producer-level max 256 UTF-8 bytes (LOCK-005/LOCK-006) — oversized detail fails closed and emits no artifact; shared schema-v1 validator unchanged (producer guard, not schema-version change). See §7.2-5/6 for the fixed `s0`/`s1`/`s1wide`-suffixed metric/gate IDs.

**5 — Timings (isolated; correctness before timing; closed per-scenario IDs)**
- Measure **only** `backup_ms_<suffix>` around the **awaited public `backup` call through published local archive** and **only** `restore_ms_<suffix>` around the **awaited public `restore` call through extraction/validation/staging and captured terminal request inside `preExitCleanup`** (post-staging parity is inside the callback per §7.2-2); each scenario emits the fixed suffix `s0`/`s1`/`s1wide` (e.g., `backup_ms_s0`/`restore_ms_s0` for `M8-S0`). All timing values are **finite numeric `metrics`**; no path/content fields.
- **Exclude**: fixture generation, DB migration/seeding, manager construction, archive metadata inspection before the call, and final cleanup. `restore_ms_<suffix>` includes callback parity because parity is required inside `preExitCleanup` (LOCK-004). Pre-staging validation failures require zero `relaunch`/`exit` (no timing artifact); callback parity failure blocks cleanup/relaunch and emits no artifact. **Per-sample fixture construction and archive/staging cleanup are outside timed intervals**; cleanup occurs in `finally` after the sample.
- **Warmup=1, samples=5 per scenario** with **unique archive/root lifecycle**: `BackupManager` local publish is **no-clobber** (existing destination file is not overwritten), so **every warmup/measured sample must use a fresh per-sample root (preferred) with unique archive path** `<root>/destination/backup-<suffix>-<sampleIndex>.zip` under its `<root>/scenario-<suffix>/sample-<index>/` (see §7.2-3/10). **No result artifact is emitted until all samples/gates pass**; any parity/completeness failure aborts before artifact emission. **No threshold gate**. Do **not** claim isolated substage timings unless separately designed. Correctness/parity gates must pass before timing is considered (bounded declared scale, no path/content/user data).

**6 — Metadata metrics / gates (inspect synthetic archive central directory and `metadata.json` only; closed per-scenario IDs)**
- Emit **finite numeric metrics only** with **fixed per-scenario ID suffixes** `s0`/`s1`/`s1wide`: `metadata_bytes_<suffix>` (metadata JSON byte length), `archive_entry_count_<suffix>` (unique central-directory entry count), `archive_compressed_bytes_<suffix>` (compressed archive byte length — synthetic artifact only); e.g., `metadata_bytes_s0` for `M8-S0`. No path/content fields. Capture `metadata_version` as **numeric `benchmark.scale` dimension or correctness gate `detail` (bounded non-empty string when present, M8 producer-level max 256 UTF-8 bytes)**, not a free-form path/content field. `benchmark.scale` fields and `metrics[].value` are finite numeric; `gates[].passed` is boolean and `gates[].detail` when present is bounded non-empty string with M8 producer-level max 256 UTF-8 bytes (LOCK-005/LOCK-006) — oversized detail fails closed and emits no artifact; shared schema-v1 validator unchanged (producer guard, not schema-version change).
- **Required correctness gates (all M8 gates are correctness; no M8 threshold gate)** — fixed per-scenario gate IDs suffixed `s0`/`s1`/`s1wide` (e.g., gate `metadata_v7_s0` for `M8-S0`); no arbitrary `scenario` field:
  1. `metadata v7` product/purpose/version (`l3ArchiveMetadata.ts:21-173` shape; `BackupManager.ts:653-671` filtering context) — `metadata_v7_<suffix>`
  2. Archive safety validation (`zipSecurityValidation.ts:96-324` containment/limits) — `archive_safety_<suffix>`
  3. Authoritative `chat.db` presence in archive — `chat_db_presence_<suffix>`
  4. Excluded promotion/WAL artifact **absence** (`BackupManager.ts:653-671`) — `excluded_absence_<suffix>`
  5. Snapshot / readonly DB integrity (`backup.ts:40-189` backup snapshot + integrity validation) — `snapshot_integrity_<suffix>`
  6. Staged restore parity (structural, §7.2-7; **post-staging parity inside `preExitCleanup` callback**; callback failure blocks cleanup/relaunch) — `restore_parity_<suffix>`
  7. Sample completeness (`warmup=1` + `samples=5` per scenario; all samples accounted) — `sample_completeness_<suffix>`
- Any parity/completeness failure **aborts before artifact emission**; **pre-staging validation failures require zero `relaunch`/`exit`; callback parity failure inside `preExitCleanup` blocks cleanup/relaunch and emits no artifact**; no artifact on failed task/parity. All gate `detail` values are bounded to max 256 UTF-8 bytes (M8 producer-level, LOCK-006 — oversized detail fails closed and emits no artifact; shared schema-v1 validator unchanged, producer guard not schema-version change) and contain no path/content/user-data.

**7 — Round-trip parity (synthetic, content-free; privacy-safe; callback-ordered)**
- Compare **only** synthetic, content-free structural `digest`/`count` and **byte digests** of fixture classes: **DB logical sample/row counts**, `Data`/`Files` **aggregate byte/hash**, IndexedDB/Local Storage **aggregate byte/hash**, and `metadata` version/product/purpose. **Never** serialize message text, entry names, absolute paths, raw user data, credentials, or a per-user archive fingerprint.
- **Deterministic fixture/parity recipe (LOCK-007, §7.2-10)**: for **each scenario and sample**, use a **fresh realpath-canonicalized root** `<root>/scenario-<suffix>/sample-<index>/` (see §7.2-3/5); seed topics `t0..t(T-1)` with messages allocated **round-robin across topics**, message IDs `m0..m(M-1)`, and **exactly one block per message**, block IDs `b0..b(M-1)` — thus expected counts **topic=T, message=M, block=M**; use the **existing schema-v1 migration and existing production repository APIs** — do not insert raw SQL outside fixture setup if repository APIs can express it; generate deterministic file payload bytes with byte value `(fileIndex + byteOffset) mod 251`; create **exactly `fileEntries` files** in synthetic `Data/Files`, each with deterministic length `floor(fileBytes/fileEntries)` plus remainder distributed one byte to the first `fileBytes mod fileEntries` files; use **fixed synthetic file IDs only inside the fixture, never emit names**; generate deterministic IndexedDB and Local Storage sentinel payloads with the **same byte rule** and aggregate them by **byte count + streaming SHA-256 over canonical concatenation in sorted fixed plane order**; use **SHA-256 for Data/Files aggregate and DB logical sample digest too**; digest input must be a **fixed canonical sequence of numeric IDs/counts/lengths and bytes, never raw message text**, sorted by **numeric fixture IDs**; **do not emit digests themselves unless the existing schema-v1 privacy policy permits opaque synthetic digests — prefer emit only parity gate pass/detail with bounded counts, not digest values** (LOCK-005/LOCK-006; `gates[].detail` max 256 UTF-8 bytes, oversized fails closed and emits no artifact).
- **Exact parity expectations (fail-closed)**: backup archive must contain **authoritative `chat.db`**, metadata **v7 `Cherry Chat`/`l3-backup`**, **no excluded promotion/WAL artifacts**; after restore staging, compare **DB T/M/M**, aggregate **Data/Files byte count + SHA-256**, **IndexedDB byte count + SHA-256**, **Local Storage byte count + SHA-256**, and **metadata version/product/purpose**; **any mismatch is a correctness failure**; callback failure blocks `relaunch`/`exit` and artifact emission (see §7.2-2/11). **Do not claim startup promotion.**
- **Parity ordering (fail-closed)**: **pre-staging validation failures** (archive safety, metadata shape, `chat.db` presence, excluded absence, snapshot integrity) **require zero `relaunch`/`exit` requests**; **post-staging parity is performed inside the restore `preExitCleanup` callback** (because `BackupManager.restoreDirect` invokes `preExitCleanup` before `app.relaunch`/`app.exit`). **On callback parity failure, cleanup/relaunch must be blocked and no artifact emitted**. Any parity or completeness failure aborts before artifact emission. **Staging parity does not prove post-relaunch startup promotion** — real relaunch/startup promotion is explicitly **not** measured or proven by M8.

**8 — Artifact (reuse closed schema v1; gitignored; unique; emit-after-all-successful; closed scenario mapping)**
- Reuse `src/main/services/chatDb/__tests__/benchResult.ts` **schemaVersion=1** and **`emitBenchmarkResultAfterSuccessfulTasks` at file-level `afterAll`**; output **only** to `test-results/bench-results` (**already gitignored**), **unique filename** (`<benchmark.id>-<YYYYMMDD-HHmmss.SSS>.json` with `-1`/`-2` suffix on collision), explicit **canonical command string** (no argv path), `git`/`node`/`pnpm`/ABI provenance, fixed numeric `scale` (`scenarioCount=3` plus per-scenario fixed `topics`/`messages`/`blocks`/`fileEntries`/`fileBytes` identified by suffix `s0`/`s1`/`s1wide`) and **fixed per-scenario `metrics`/`gates` with ID suffixes `s0`/`s1`/`s1wide` (e.g., `backup_ms_s0`, `metadata_bytes_s0`, gate `restore_parity_s0`) and **bounded gate `detail` (M8 producer-level max 256 UTF-8 bytes, LOCK-006 — oversized detail fails closed and emits no artifact; shared schema-v1 validator unchanged)**.
- **No new artifact schema, no raw DB size/profile/path/`scenario` fields, no artifact on failed task/parity (including callback parity failure inside `preExitCleanup`)**. Schema remains the closed set of `performance-measurement.md` §3 (`benchmark.scale` finite numeric fields and `metrics[].value` finite numeric; `gates[].passed` boolean and `gates[].detail` bounded non-empty string when present with M8 producer-level max 256 UTF-8 bytes, LOCK-005/LOCK-006 — oversized detail fails closed and emits no artifact; shared schema-v1 validator unchanged, producer guard not schema-version change).

**11 — Failure lifecycle detail (restoreDirect ordering)**

- `BackupManager.restoreDirect` **removes `extractionDir` before invoking `preExitCleanup`** (`src/main/services/BackupManager.ts:1634-1775`); in the future M8 harness, staged parity runs **inside `preExitCleanup`** (LOCK-004), so **callback parity failure occurs after extraction cleanup has already completed**; staged `.restore` directories are **not rolled back by this path** and `app.relaunch`/`app.exit` are **not called**.
- Harness must **inspect/record this post-extraction-cleanup state** (extraction removed, `.restore` present/unrolled-back, no relaunch/exit), **remove all sample-owned roots in `finally`**, and **emit no artifact** on callback failure. **Pre-staging validation failures produce zero `relaunch`/`exit` and no artifact**. See §7.2-2/5/10.

Any historical observation of `node v22.23.1` (pre-correction observation) does not satisfy the pinned requirement and must be corrected to the pinned version before execution.
